\left\lvert\bm{\epsilon}_{8,(i),\tau}\right\rvert
, we know that,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\sum_{i=1}^{N}\text{Tr}\left(\bm{R}^{T}_{(i)}\left(\Delta\bm{U}_{\tau}\Delta\bm{U}_{\tau}^{T}+\Delta\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\Delta\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}^{T}\right)\bm{F}_{(i)}\right)\right\rvert+\left\lvert\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\alpha=1}^{8}\bm{\epsilon}_{\alpha,(i),\tau}\right\rvert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\left\lVert\Delta\bm{U}_{\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+\left\lVert\Delta\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\right)\left\lVert\bm{R}_{(i)}\right\rVert\left\lVert\hat{\bm{F}}_{(i)}\right\rVert+14\sqrt{2}G_{max,op}\sqrt{\widetilde{\zeta}_{(i),\tau}}\left(\zeta_{(i),\tau}+\zeta_{(0),\tau}\right)

From Lemma
18
, we know that
\left\lVert\bm{R}_{(i)}\right\rVert\left\lVert\hat{\bm{F}}_{(i)}\right\rVert\leq\frac{\hat{\mu}\hat{\theta}}{64}
, we can thus upper bound the first term as,

\displaystyle 2\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\left\lVert\Delta\bm{U}_{\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+\left\lVert\Delta\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\right)\left\lVert\bm{R}_{(i)}\right\rVert\left\lVert\hat{\bm{F}}_{(i)}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\left\lVert\Delta\bm{U}_{\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+\left\lVert\Delta\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\right)\frac{\hat{\mu}\hat{\theta}}{32}

\displaystyle\leq\phi_{\tau}/2

where the last inequality comes from Lemma
19
.

Also, the second term can be bounded as,

\displaystyle 14\sqrt{2}G_{max,op}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sqrt{\widetilde{\zeta}_{(i),\tau}}\left(\zeta_{(i),\tau}+\zeta_{(0),\tau}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 14\sqrt{2}G_{max,op}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sqrt{\widetilde{\zeta}_{(i),\tau}}\sqrt{\zeta_{(i),\tau}+\zeta_{(0),\tau}}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{N}\zeta_{(j),\tau}+\zeta_{(0),\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq 14\sqrt{2}G_{max,op}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\widetilde{\zeta}_{(i),\tau}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\zeta_{(i),\tau}+\zeta_{(0),\tau}}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{N}\zeta_{(j),\tau}+\zeta_{(0),\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq 14\sqrt{2}G_{max,op}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\zeta_{(i),\tau}+\zeta_{(0),\tau}\right)^{1.5}

\displaystyle\leq 14\sqrt{2}G_{max,op}\left(2\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert\Delta\bm{U}_{\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+\left\lVert\Delta\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\right)^{1.5}

\displaystyle\leq 56G_{max,op}\left(\frac{\phi_{\tau}}{\frac{\hat{\mu}\hat{\theta}}{16}}\right)^{3/2}\leq\phi_{\tau}/2

where the second inequality comes from Cauchy-Schwrtz inequality, the third inequality comes from Lemma
13
, the fourth inequality comes from Lemma
26
, the fifth inequality comes from Lemma
19
, and the last inequality comes from the fact that
\phi_{\tau}\leq\frac{\hat{\mu}^{3}\hat{\theta}^{3}}{51380224G_{max,op}^{2}}
.

This completes the proof.

∎

Combining Lemma
19
with Lemma
20
, we can prove the following PL-inequality.

Lemma 21
.

(Lemma
12
in the main paper)
Under the same conditions as Theorem
10
, we have

\displaystyle N\left\lVert\square\bm{U}_{\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert\square\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\geq\frac{\hat{\theta}\hat{\mu}}{16}\phi_{\tau}

Proof.

From Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, we know that,

\displaystyle-N\left\langle\square\bm{U}_{\tau},\Delta\bm{U}_{\tau}\right\rangle-\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\langle\square\bm{V}_{(i),\tau},\Delta\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq N\left\lVert\square\bm{U}_{\tau}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\Delta\bm{U}_{\tau}\right\rVert+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert\square\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\Delta\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{N\left\lVert\square\bm{U}_{\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert\square\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}}\sqrt{N\left\lVert\Delta\bm{U}_{\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert\Delta\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{N\left\lVert\square\bm{U}_{\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert\square\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{\tau}}{\frac{\hat{\mu}\hat{\theta}}{16}}}

where the last inequality comes from Lemma
19
.

From Lemma
20
, we know,

\displaystyle-N\left\langle\square\bm{U}_{\tau},\Delta\bm{U}_{\tau}\right\rangle-\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\langle\square\bm{V}_{(i),\tau},\Delta\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\phi_{\tau}

Combining them, we have,

\displaystyle N\left\lVert\square\bm{U}_{\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert\square\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\geq\frac{\hat{\theta}\hat{\mu}}{16}\phi_{\tau}

∎

Finally, we come to the proof of Theorem
10
:

Proof.

Combining Lemma
21
with equation (
64
), we know:

\displaystyle-f\left(\bm{U}_{\tau+1},\{\bm{V}_{(i),\tau+1}\}\right)\leq-f\left(\bm{U}_{\tau},\{\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\}\right)-\frac{\eta}{2}\frac{\hat{\mu}\hat{\theta}}{16}

We add
f^{\star}
on both sides. Since
\phi_{\tau}=f^{\star}--f\left(\bm{U}_{\tau},\{\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}\}\right)
, we have:

\displaystyle\phi_{\tau+1}

\displaystyle\leq\phi_{\tau}-\frac{\eta}{2}\frac{\hat{\mu}\hat{\theta}}{16}\phi_{\tau}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{\eta\theta\mu}{64}\right)\phi_{\tau}

Thus
\phi_{\tau}
decreases linearly with
\tau
. From Lemma
19
and Lemma
26
, we can show
\left\lVert\bm{P}_{\bm{U}_{\tau}}-\hat{\bm{\Pi}}_{g}\right\rVert_{F}
and
\left\lVert\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i),\tau}}-\hat{\bm{\Pi}}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{F}
decrease linearly to zero as well.

This completes the proof of Theorem
10
.
∎

Appendix F
Some examples of generalized retraction

In this section, we discuss two popular normalization schemes: polar projection and QR decomposition. We prove that both fit the Definition
5
of a generalized retraction. The analysis in this section is inspired by
Liu et al. 2019
. However,
Liu et al. 2019
only considers conventional retraction operations, while we consider generalized retractions.

F.1
Polar projection

Polar projection is defined as:

\mathcal{GR}^{\text{polar}}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)=\left(\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}\right)\left(\bm{I}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}

Then obviously,

col(\mathcal{GR}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right))=col\left(\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}\right)

To verify the second property, we can calculate the difference between
\mathcal{GR}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)
and
\bm{U}+\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}_{\bm{U}}}(\bm{\xi})
.

Notice that

\displaystyle\left(\bm{I}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=\bm{I}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)^{n}\frac{(2n-1)!!(-1)^{n}}{2^{n}n!}

We have

\displaystyle\mathcal{GR}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)-(\bm{U}+\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}_{\bm{U}}}(\bm{\xi}))

(82)

\displaystyle=\left(\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}\right)\left(\bm{I}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)^{n}\frac{(2n-1)!!(-1)^{n}}{2^{n}n!}\right)

\displaystyle-\left(\bm{U}-\bm{\xi}+\frac{1}{2}\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+\frac{1}{2}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}+\left(\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}\right)\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)^{n}\frac{(2n-1)!!(-1)^{n}}{2^{n}n!}\right)

By the property of Frobinius norm:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\bm{U}^{T}\right\rVert_{op}+\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=2\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}+\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}

Therefore,

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{GR}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)-(\bm{U}+\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}_{\bm{U}}}(\bm{\xi}))\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}+\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}\right\rVert_{F}+\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\left\lVert\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}^{n}\frac{(2n-1)!!}{2^{n}n!}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}^{3}+\left(1+\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\left(3\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}\right)^{n}\frac{(2n-1)!!}{2^{n}n!}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}^{3}+\left(1+\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\frac{3\left(3\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}\right)^{2}+\left(3\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}\right)^{3}}{2}

\displaystyle\leq M_{polar}\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}

where
M_{polar}=\frac{253}{8}
. We applied the following summation in the derivation:

\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}x^{n}\frac{(2n-1)!!}{2^{n}n!}

\displaystyle=(1-x)^{-1/2}-(1+\frac{x}{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{3x^{2}+x^{3}}{\sqrt{1-x}+(1-x)(1+\frac{x}{2})}

and the fact that
x\leq\frac{1}{2}
in the third inequality.

Since

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}_{\bm{U}}}(\bm{\xi})+\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{N}_{\bm{U}}}(\bm{\xi})\right\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}_{\bm{U}}}(\bm{\xi})\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{N}_{\bm{U}}}(\bm{\xi})\right\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}_{\bm{U}}}(\bm{\xi})\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{N}_{\bm{U}}}(\bm{\xi})\right\rVert_{F}

We prove that polar projection is a generalized retraction with
M_{1}=\frac{253}{4}
and
M_{2}=\frac{253}{8}
.

Polar projection can be implemented via singular value decomposition of
\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}
, whose computational complexity is
O(dr^{2}+r^{3})

(
Breloy et al. 2021
)
.

F.2
QR decomposition

QR decomposition is an extension of Gram-Schmidt orthonormalization. For a matrix
\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
, the method finds a orthogonal matrix
\bm{Q}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
and an upper triangular matrix
\bm{R}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
, such that
\bm{Q}\bm{R}=\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}
. Then
\mathcal{GR}^{\text{QR}}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)=\bm{Q}
.

In this section, we will prove that QR decomposition is a generalized retraction for
\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{4}
. Our proof in this section extends that in
Liu et al. 2019
.

Notice that
col(\bm{U}+\bm{\xi})=col(\bm{Q})
, thus the first property of generalized retraction in Definition
5
is satisfied. We will prove the second in the case
M_{3}=\frac{1}{4}

Similar to
Liu et al. 2019
, we define
\bm{U}(t)=\bm{U}+t\bm{\xi}
, for
t\in[0,1]
, and use
\bm{Q}(t)\bm{R}(t)
to denote the QR decomposition of
\bm{U}(t)
. Then:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{GR}^{\text{QR}}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)-\left(\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}\right)\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\bm{Q}(1)-\bm{Q}(1)\bm{R}(1)\right\rVert_{F}=\left\lVert\bm{Q}(1)\left(\bm{I}-\bm{R}(1)\right)\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\bm{R}(1)-\bm{R}(0)\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\int_{0}^{1}\bm{R}^{{}^{\prime}}(t)dt\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{1}\left\lVert\bm{R}^{{}^{\prime}}(t)\right\rVert_{F}dt

Since
\bm{Q}(t)\bm{R}(t)
is the QR decomposition of
\bm{U}(t)
, we have:

\bm{R}^{T}(t)\bm{R}(t)=\bm{U}^{T}(t)\bm{U}(t)=\bm{U}^{T}\bm{U}+t\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+t\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+t^{2}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}

(83)

Taking the derivative with respect to
t
on both sides, we have:

\displaystyle\left(\bm{R}^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{T}(t)\bm{R}(t)+\bm{R}^{T}(t)\bm{R}^{{}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle=\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+2t\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}

We can left multiply both sides by
\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)
, and right multiply both sides by
\bm{R}^{-1}(t)
, to obtain:

\displaystyle\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)\left(\bm{R}^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{T}(t)+\bm{R}^{{}^{\prime}}(t)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)=\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)\left(\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+2t\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)

Since on the left hand side,
\bm{R}^{{}^{\prime}}(t)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)
is an upper triangular matrix, its transpose
\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)\left(\bm{R}^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{T}(t)
is a lower triangular matrix, we have:

\bm{R}^{{}^{\prime}}(t)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)=\text{up}\left[\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)\left(\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+2t\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)\right]

where for
\bm{C}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
,
\text{up}\left[\cdot\right]
is defined as:

\text{up}\left[\bm{C}\right]_{ij}=\left\{\begin{aligned} &C_{ij}\text{, if }j>i\\
&\frac{1}{2}C_{ii}\text{, if }j=i\\
&0\text{, if }j<i\\
\end{aligned}\right.

Therefore,

\bm{R}^{{}^{\prime}}(t)=\text{up}\left[\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)\left(\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+2t\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)\right]\bm{R}(t)

and accordingly:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{R}^{{}^{\prime}}(t)\right\rVert_{F}=\left\lVert\text{up}\left[\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)\left(\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+2t\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)\right]\bm{R}(t)\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\text{up}\left[\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)\left(\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+2t\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)\right]\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\bm{R}(t)\right\rVert_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)\left(\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+2t\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\bm{R}(t)\right\rVert_{op}

where we used Lemma
23
for the first inequality.

From (
83
), we know that:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{R}(t)\right\rVert_{op}^{2}=\left\lVert\bm{R}(t)^{T}\bm{R}(t)\right\rVert_{op}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\bm{I}+t\left(\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)+t^{2}\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{op}

\displaystyle\geq 1-t\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{op}-t^{2}\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{op}

\displaystyle\geq 1-2\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}-\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{7}{16}

where the first inequality comes from the triangle inequality, the second comes from the fact that
\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{F}\geq\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{op}
, and the third comes from the requirement
\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}\leq\frac{1}{4}
.

Similarly, we can derive:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{R}(t)\right\rVert_{op}^{2}=\left\lVert\bm{R}(t)^{T}\bm{R}(t)\right\rVert_{op}\leq 1+2\left\lVert\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}+\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}\leq\frac{25}{16}

As a result,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{R}^{{}^{\prime}}(t)\right\rVert_{F}\leq\left\lVert\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)\left(\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}+2t\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\bm{R}(t)\right\rVert_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\left\lVert\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)\left(\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)\right\rVert_{F}+\left\lVert\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)\left(2t\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)\right\rVert_{F}\right)\frac{5}{4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5}{4}\left(\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)\right\rVert_{op}+2t\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\left(\bm{R}^{-1}\right)^{T}(t)\bm{R}^{-1}(t)\right\rVert_{op}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{20}{7}\left(\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}+2t\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}\right)

Hence,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{GR}^{\text{QR}}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)-\left(\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}\right)\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{20}{7}\left(\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}+\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}\right)

Since
\bm{U}
is an orthogonal matrix,
\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{N}_{\bm{U}}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)\right\rVert_{F}=\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert\bm{U}\left(\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right)\right\rVert_{F}=\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{U}+\bm{U}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}
. By Cauchy-Schwartz inequality,
\left\lVert\bm{\xi}^{T}\bm{\xi}\right\rVert_{F}=\left\lVert\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{N}_{\bm{U}}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)+\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}_{\bm{U}}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)\right)^{T}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{N}_{\bm{U}}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)+\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}_{\bm{U}}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)\right)\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{N}_{\bm{U}}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)\right\rVert_{F}^{2}+2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}_{\bm{U}}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)\right\rVert_{F}^{2}
.

Thus we have:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{GR}^{\text{QR}}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)-\left(\bm{U}+\bm{\xi}\right)\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{20}{7}\left(2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{N}_{\bm{U}}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)\right\rVert_{F}+2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{N}_{\bm{U}}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)\right\rVert^{2}_{F}+2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}_{\bm{U}}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{80}{7}\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{N}_{\bm{U}}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)\right\rVert_{F}+\frac{40}{7}\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}_{\bm{U}}}\left(\bm{\xi}\right)\right\rVert_{F}^{2}

Hence the second property of definition holds with
M_{1}=\frac{40}{7}
and
M_{2}=\frac{80}{7}
.

QR decomposition can be implemented by Gram-Schmidt or Householder algorithm with computation complexity of
O(dr^{2})

Appendix G
Auxiliary lemmas

In this section, we show some auxiliary lemmas needed for the proof in earlier Sections. Most lemmas are derived from basic facts in linear algebra.

We begin with some general inequalities related to matrix trace norms.

Lemma 22
.

For two matrices
\bm{A},\bm{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
, if both
\bm{A},\bm{B}
are symmetric positive definite, then:

\text{Tr}\left(\bm{A}\bm{B}\right)\geq 0

A simple corollary is that if
\bm{A}_{1},\bm{A}_{2},\bm{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
are symmetric and
\bm{B}
is positive semi-definite, and
\bm{A}_{1}\succeq\bm{A}_{2}
, then

\text{Tr}\left(\bm{A}_{1}\bm{B}\right)\geq\text{Tr}\left(\bm{A}_{2}\bm{B}\right)

Proof.

Since both
\bm{A}
and
\bm{B}
are positive symmetric, there exists
\bm{X},\bm{Y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
, such that
A=X^{T}X
and
\bm{B}=\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{Y}
, therefore:

\displaystyle\text{Tr}\left(\bm{A}\bm{B}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left(\bm{X}^{T}\bm{X}\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{Y}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left((\bm{Y}\bm{X}^{T})^{T}\bm{Y}\bm{X}^{T}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 0

∎

The following lemma presents an upper bound of the Frobenius norm of the product of two matrices.

Lemma 23
.

For two matrices
\bm{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}
, and
\bm{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times k}
, we have:

\left\lVert\bm{A}\bm{B}\right\rVert_{F}\leq\left\lVert\bm{A}\right\rVert_{op}\left\lVert\bm{B}\right\rVert_{F}

and:

\left\lVert\bm{A}\bm{B}\right\rVert_{F}\leq\left\lVert\bm{A}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\bm{B}\right\rVert_{op}

The proof of the lemma can be found in
Sun and Luo 2015
.

The following lemma introduces a simple upper bound on the Frobenius norm of
\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}
.

Lemma 24
.

For any rank-r orthonormal matrix
\bm{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
, and rank-r projection matrix
\bm{P}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
, we have:

\left\lVert\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right\rVert_{F}\leq r-\text{Tr}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)

(84)

Proof.

It is easy to see that
\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}
is positive semidefinite. Also, for a positive semidefinite matrix, its Frobenius norm is upper bounded by its trace. Inequality (
84
) follows accordingly.
∎

We can proceed to the following lemma that upper bounds the trace of the
k
-th power of
\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}
.

Lemma 25
.

For any rank-r orthonormal matrix
\bm{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
, and rank-r projection matrix
\bm{P}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
,
k=1,2,...
,
\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{k}
is positive semi-definite and:

0\leq Tr\left(\left(\bm{I}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{k}\right)\leq\left(r-\text{Tr}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)\right)^{k}

(85)

Proof.

Since
\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}
is symmetric positive semidefinite,
\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{k}
is also symmetric positive semidefinite. Assume eigenvalues of
\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}
are
\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2},\cdots,\lambda_{d}
, with
\lambda_{1}\geq\lambda_{2}\geq\cdots\geq\lambda_{d}
, we know that
Tr\left(\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{k}\right)=\sum_{i=1}^{d}\lambda_{i}^{k}\leq\lambda_{1}^{k-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\lambda_{i}
.

By Lemma
24
, we know that

\lambda_{1}^{k-1}\leq\left\lVert\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right\rVert_{F}^{k-1}\leq\left(r-\text{Tr}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)\right)^{k-1}

This completes our proof.
∎

Based on the above results, we can discuss some properties of the projection of a matrix onto a subspace. Suppose we know the column space of
\bm{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
is close to that of
\bm{P}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
, can we find a matrix
\bm{U}^{*}
close to
\bm{U}
with column vectors in
col(\bm{P})
? The following two lemmas give affirmative answers.

Lemma 26
.

For any rank-r orthonormal matrix
\bm{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
, and rank-r projection matrix
\bm{P}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
, we define:

\bm{U}^{\star}=\bm{P}\bm{U}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{-1/2}

If
r-\text{Tr}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)\leq 1
, we have:

\left\lVert\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\geq r-\text{Tr}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)

(86)

and,

\left\lVert\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\leq 2\left(r-\text{Tr}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)\right)

(87)

Proof.

To prove the lower bound (
86
) and upper bound (
87
), we can write
\left\lVert\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}
as,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}=\left\langle\bm{U},\bm{U}\right\rangle+\left\langle\bm{U}^{\star},\bm{U}^{\star}\right\rangle-2\left\langle\bm{U},\bm{U}^{\star}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=2r-2\left\langle\bm{U},\bm{U}^{\star}\right\rangle

We first find an upper bound for
\left\langle\bm{U},\bm{U}^{\star}\right\rangle
.

Notice that:

\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{U},\bm{U}^{\star}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{U}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)\right)^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)-\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\frac{(2n-3)!!}{2^{n}n!}\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{n}\right)

\displaystyle=r-\frac{1}{2}\text{Tr}\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)-\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\frac{(2n-3)!!}{2^{n}n!}\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{n}\right)

\displaystyle\leq r-\frac{1}{2}\text{Tr}\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)

We used the series
(1-x)^{\frac{1}{2}}=1-\frac{1}{2}x-\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\frac{(2n-3)!!}{2^{n}n!}x^{n}
, and the result
\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{n}\right)\geq 0
from Lemma
25
.

As a result:

\left\lVert\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\geq\text{Tr}\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)=r-\text{Tr}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)

Similarly, from Lemma
25
,
\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{n}\right)\leq\text{Tr}\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{n}
, thus:

\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{U},\bm{U}^{\star}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=r-\frac{1}{2}\text{Tr}\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)-\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\frac{(2n-3)!!}{2^{n}n!}\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{n}\right)

\displaystyle\geq r-\frac{1}{2}\text{Tr}\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)-\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\frac{(2n-3)!!}{2^{n}n!}\text{Tr}\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)^{n}

\displaystyle=r+\left(1-\text{Tr}\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}-1

\displaystyle\geq-\text{Tr}\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)

where we used the relation
\sqrt{1-x}-1\geq-x,\forall x\in[0,1]
, in the last inequality.

Thus

\left\lVert\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\leq 2\text{Tr}\left(\bm{I}_{r}-\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)=2\left(r-\text{Tr}\left(\bm{U}^{T}\bm{P}\bm{U}\right)\right)

This completes our proof.
∎

The following lemma shows that we can identify global PCs from local PCs.

Lemma 27
.

Suppose for
i=1,\cdots,N
,
\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}
,
\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}
and
\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}
,
\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}
are projection matrices satisfying
\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}=0
and
\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}=0
for each
i
. Among them,
\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}
and
\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}
have rank
r_{1}
,
\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}
and
\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}
have rank
r_{2,(i)}
. If there exists a positive constant
\theta>0
such that

\lambda_{max}(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star})\leq 1-\theta

we have the following bound:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}

\displaystyle r_{1}+r_{2,(i)}-Tr\left(\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}+\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\right)\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}+\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\right)

(88)

\displaystyle\leq N\left(r_{1}-Tr(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}})\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{N}r_{2,(i)}-Tr(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}})

And also:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}r_{1}+r_{2,(i)}-Tr\left(\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}+\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\right)\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}+\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\right)

(89)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\theta}{2}\left(N\left(r_{1}-Tr(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}})\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{N}r_{2,(i)}-Tr(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}})\right)

Notice that we can replace
+
by
\oplus
on the left hand side of (
88
) and (
89
)

Proof.

We first calculate the upper bound.

Since
\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}
is positive semidefinite, we know that:

\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\right)\geq 0

Thus

\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\right)=\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\geq 0

Similarly, we have:

\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\right)\geq 0

Combining them, we have:

\displaystyle\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}+\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\right)\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}+\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)

This proves inequality (
88
).

Next, we calculate the lower bound.

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}+\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\right)\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}+\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{N}\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star})\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{N}\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{N}\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)

Since
\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)
and
\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}
are both symmetric positive semidefinite, we have:

\displaystyle\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\right)\lambda_{max}\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\left(1-\theta\right)

\displaystyle=\left(r_{1}-\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\right)\left(1-\theta\right)

For notation simplicity, we define
z_{0}=r_{1}-\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)
and
z_{i}=r_{2,(i)}-\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)
.

From the orthogonality, we have:

\displaystyle\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\right)\lambda_{max}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}-\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle=z_{i}

Also, from the orthogonality, we have:

\displaystyle\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\right)\right)\lambda_{max}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle=z_{0}

Combining the two:

\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\leq\min\{z_{0},z_{i}\}

As a result:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}r_{1}+r_{2,(i)}

\displaystyle-\left[\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}\left(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}}^{\star}\right)+\text{Tr}\left(\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}\bm{P}_{\bm{V}_{(i)}}^{\star}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{i=1}^{N}z_{0}-\left(1-\theta\right)z_{0}+z_{i}-\min\{z_{0},z_{i}\}

Since for any number
\nu\in\left(0,1\right)
, we know:

z_{i}-\min\{z_{0},z_{i}\}\geq\nu\left(z_{i}-z_{0}\right)

We can set
\nu=\frac{\theta}{2}
, then

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}z_{0}-\left(1-\theta\right)z_{0}+z_{i}-\min\{z_{0},z_{i}\}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{i=1}^{N}\theta z_{0}+\frac{\theta}{2}\left(z_{i}-z_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\theta}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{N}z_{0}+z_{i}

This proves inequality (
89
).
∎
